Let's define correlate(a, v) in numpy: doc: result[k] = sum_i a[i] * v[i+k - (n2-1)]? Actually for 1D: if a length M, v length N. full length M+N-1. For k=0..N-1: result[k] = ∑_{i=0}^{N-1-k} a[i+k] * v[i]? For k=N-1..M+N-2: result[k]=∑_{i=0}^{M-N+(k-(N-1))} a[i] * v[i+k-(N-1)]? If M=N=n:
- For k=0..n-1: result[k]=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-k} a[i+k] v[i].
- For k=n-1..2n-2: let ℓ=k-(n-1)=0..n-1: result[k]=∑_{i=0}^{n-1-ℓ} a[i] v[i+ℓ].
Here a=h, v=1-h.
So:
for ℓ≥0, positive result index n-1+ℓ: S_ℓ^+ = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-ℓ} h[i] (1-h[i+ℓ]).
for ℓ≥0, negative result index n-1-ℓ: S_ℓ^- = ∑_{i=0}^{n-1-ℓ} h[i+ℓ] (1-h[i]).
For ℓ=0 both same.
C = max over ℓ=0..n-1 of dx*max(S_ℓ^+, S_ℓ^-). (index 0? full length is 2n-1, indices 0..2n-2; n-1 is lag0; n-1-ℓ for ℓ>0 are negative lags.) Good.

Derivative:

So implement gradient_full(x):
  S_raw = np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
  C = S_raw.max()
  shift=beta*(S_raw-C); exps=np.exp(-np.logaddexp?); Since shift≤0, exps=np.exp(shift) safe (maybe underflow). sume=exps.sum(); weights=exps/sume.
  val=C + np.log(sume)/beta
  G = zeros(n); G += weights[n-1]*(1-2*x)
  for ell in 1..n-1:
    wp=weights[n-1+ell]; wn=weights[n-1-ell]; c=wp+wn
    if c !=0:
      G[:n-ell] += wp - c*x[ell:]
      G[ell:] += wn - c*x[:-ell]
  return val, G
This is exact gradient of F with full LSE. Check derivative of F: F = C + (1/β)log∑exp(β(S-C)). If C changes? C = max S, not differentiable where max active? But F derivative formula using weights normalized over e^{β(S-C)}? Let's derive: LSE e = β^{-1} log∑e^{βS_j}. If we write C=log sum? We used C=max S, then F=C + β^{-1} log∑e^{β(S_j-C)}. This equals β^{-1} log(∑e^{βS_j}) (since C cancels) exactly. Derivative = ∑ (e^{βS_j}/∑e) ∇S_j. No derivative of C. Good. C in value just for numerical stability; function equals LSE/beta. So gradient weights normalized over all exp(shift). Correct.

Let's implement a cleaner O(n) delta using the flip V formula (per p) and update C.

Function flip_delta(p, h, n): returns delta array length m for flipping p from 0 to1. We can compute O(n):
  delta = zeros(m)
  for ell in 1..n-1:
    if p < n-ell:
      delta[n-1+ell] += 1 - h[p+ell]
      delta[n-1-ell] -= h[p+ell]
    if p >= ell:
      delta[n-1+ell] -= h[p-ell]
      delta[n-1-ell] += 1 - h[p-ell]
  return delta
This loop 400 per flip, two flips 800. For candidate pair: delta = delta_i - delta_j. But computing delta_i for each zero once per sweep and delta_j for each one? We can precompute flip delta for all positions O(n^2) per sweep by matrix V as above. Then candidate delta = V[i] - V[j]. To evaluate max, (C+V[i]-V[j]).max() O(m) in C. This is fast with numpy.

Let's implement V computation function:
def compute_flip_vectors(h,n):
  m=2*n-1
  V=np.zeros((n,m), dtype=float)
  # for ell 1..n-1
  for ell in range(1,n):
    left_len = n-ell
    posP = n-1+ell
    posN = n-1-ell
    # p < left_len: p+ell = ell..n-1 h[p+ell]
    h_right = h[ell:]
    V[:left_len, posP] += 1.0 - h_right
    V[:left_len, posN] -= h_right
    h_left = h[:left_len]
    V[ell:, posP] -= h_left
    V[ell:, posN] += 1.0 - h_left
  return V
Check for ell where left_len=n-ell. V[ell:] length n-ell, posP index n-1+ell (≤2n-2). Good. Center unused.

Alternatively compute delta by directly evaluating contributions before/after for all lags ℓ in O(n), using vectorized masks per ℓ? For each ℓ, the set of pairs affected are those where one endpoint is i or j. We can update C counts by removing old contributions and adding new. There are up to 4 pairs per ℓ: (i,i±ℓ), (j,j±ℓ), plus if i±ℓ=j etc. Easier: For each ℓ=1..n-1:
  oldP = sum over i in overlap h_i(1-h_{i+ℓ}). NewP similarly. Since only h_i/h_j changed, oldP and newP differ only for terms with index a such that a∈{i,j} or a+ℓ∈{i,j}. We can compute deltaP by checking these up to 4 a values and summing old->new, avoiding double count. Similarly for N. That's O(n) with few operations per ℓ in Python; maybe 400*constant, okay for 100k swaps (40M Python ops) maybe okay.

def binary_local_search(h,n,deadline, max_sweeps=...):

smooth_fun(x,beta,dx):
   S = np.correlate(x, 1.0-x, 'full')*dx
   C = S.max() if S.size else 0
   shift = beta*(S-C)
   # avoid overflow: shift<=0; but due C maybe less? Use np.maximum? If S-C <=0. Numerical: C max exact, okay. set shift = np.where(shift < -745, -745, shift)
   e = np.exp(shift)
   sume = e.sum()
   val = C + np.log(sume)/beta
   return val

smooth_jac(x,beta,dx): recompute S? To ensure same weights, recompute. We'll duplicate code in a helper smooth_compute that returns val, grad. Then fun/jac call it. Duplicate but okay.
   n=x.size
   S_counts = np.correlate(x,1-x,'full') # counts
   S = S_counts*dx
   C = S.max()
   shift = beta*(S-C)
   e = np.exp(np.clip(shift, -745, 0.0))
   sume=e.sum()
   if sume <=0: weights zeros? but at least one.
   w = e/sume
   val=C+np.log(sume)/beta
   G = w[n-1]*(1.0-2.0*x)
   # for ell
   # We can loop ell and use local variables for speed
   for ell in range(1,n):
      wp = w[n-1+ell]
      wn = w[n-1-ell]
      c = wp+wn
      if c == 0.0: continue
      left = n-ell
      G[:left] += wp - c*x[ell:]
      G[ell:] += wn - c*x[:left]
   G *= dx
   return val, G

Need ensure G correct for weights at lag0. w[n-1] for S0. If beta large, w0 maybe negligible.

project_feasible robust:
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
  h=np.clip(h,0.0,1.0)
  target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(100):
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff) < 1e-12: break
    if diff>0:
      room=1.0-h
      mask=room>1e-15
      if not mask.any(): break
      add = diff / room[mask].sum()
      # add may >1? If diff > total room? But diff <= total room if sum< target? Could be if h clipped? Use max add clipped:
      h[mask] += add*room[mask]
    else:
      mask=h>1e-15
      if not mask.any(): break
      h[mask] *= (h.sum()+diff)/h.sum()? Or h += diff/h.sum()*h.
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # If still not close, adjust coordinates with room greedily
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    if diff>0:
      for i in np.argsort(1-h)[::-1]:
        delta=min(diff,1-h[i]); h[i]+=delta; diff-=delta; if diff<=0: break
    else:
      for i in np.argsort(h)[::-1]:
        delta=max(diff, h[i]-0?) Actually need subtract -diff up to h[i].
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # make sum as exact as possible:
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>0:
    if diff>0:
      idx=int(np.argmax(1-h)); cap=1-h[idx]; h[idx]+=min(diff,cap)
    else:
      idx=int(np.argmax(h)); cap=h[idx]; h[idx]+=max(diff,-cap) # diff negative
  return h
This may leave sum diff if no room. But target achievable if feasible.

Another powerful method: Since objective terms are bilinear and h bounds, we can solve a sequence of trust-region convex relaxations: Let T current max. For each active term, linearize around current h: g_k ≈ g_k0 + ∇g_k0·(h-h0). We want find h feasible with max linearized ≤ T - ε. This is an LP with variables h (and T) bilinear? Linearized constraints are linear! We can solve LP:
minimize T? or find direction p minimizing max_k [g_k(h0)+∇_k·p]
subject p ∈ B(h0) maybe trust region, sum p=0, h0+p∈[0,1]. If we can find p reducing max, take step. This is a linear programming problem with n variables and active constraints. We can use scipy.optimize.linprog (HiGHS) which is fast. For each iteration, choose active set A (terms with g_k ≥ max - tol). Solve LP:
minimize t
subject:
  for a in A: ∇_a·p + (g_a - t) ≤ 0  (linear in p,t)
  sum p =0
  -min(h0,i) ≤ p_i ≤ 1 - h0,i
  maximize? Also trust region on p maybe norm bound to ensure linearization valid: add diagonal quadratic not LP; can bound each p_i (e.g., |p_i|≤0.2) or ℓ∞. Use bounds.
Solution p, t. If t < current max, move h = h0 - α p? Wait p direction to reduce; constraints use p. If t < g_max, step with α=1 or line search. This is a projected linearization step (IPOPT-like). LP can be very effective for minimax linearization.
LP size: n=400, active constraints maybe up to all 799 but we can select top K (e.g., 100). Variables p (400) + t. Constraints 100 + equality. HiGHS fast. We can implement with scipy.optimize.linprog. This could be a top-level improvement over SLSQP.

We want minimize max linearized L_k = S_k + ∇_k·p over active, with sum p=0, p bounds [-h0, 1-h0], maybe trust p norm. This LP:
variables x = p (n) + t.
Minimize t.
Constraints: ∇_k·p - t ≤ -S_k for each k in A.
Equality: sum p =0.
Bounds: p_i ∈ [-h0_i,1-h0_i]. t unbounded (or [0, n]).

elif idx < n-1: ell=n-1-idx negative N_ell: grad[x] = (x >= ell)*(1 - h[x-ell]) - (x < n-ell)*h[x+ell]

Need term_grad(idx,h,n) top-level. Let's implement efficiently:
def term_grad(idx, h, n, dx):
  ell0=n-1
  g=np.zeros(n)
  if idx==ell0: g=1-2h
  elif idx>ell0:
    ell=idx-ell0
    left=n-ell
    g[:left] += 1.0 - h[ell:]
    if ell>0: g[ell:] -= h[:left]
  else:
    ell=ell0-idx
    left=n-ell
    # N: right variable p:
    g[ell:] += 1.0 - h[:left]
    g[:left] -= h[ell:]
  return g*dx
Check for N: earlier N delta: right + (1-h_left), left -h_right. So g[ell:] +=1 - h[:-ell]; g[:left] -= h[ell:]. Yes.
For P: g[:left]+=1 - h[ell:]; g[ell:] -= h[:left]. Yes.
For idx0 (full result first) negative ell=n-1: left=1; g[ell:] length1 at p=n-1 +=1-h[0]; g[:1] -=h[n-1]. OK.

def resample_h(h,n,nn):
   xo=np.linspace(0,2,n)
   xnew=np.linspace(0,2,nn)
   hh=np.interp(xnew, xo, h)
   return project_feasible(hh,nn)

Let's write `lp_minmax_step` carefully:
def lp_minmax_step(h,n,trust,active_count=200):
   dx=2/n; S=np.correlate(h,1-h,'full')*dx; M=S.max()
   # active all top active_count and those near M
   near = np.where(S >= M - 0.01)[0]
   if near.size > active_count:
       # combine top by value? Use argsort
       idx = np.argsort(S)[-active_count:]
   else:
       idx = near
   if idx.size ==0: idx=np.array([int(np.argmax(S))])
   # Ensure include max
   imax = int(np.argmax(S))
   if imax not in idx: idx=np.append(idx,imax)
   A_rows=[]; b=[]; for ix in idx: g=term_grad(ix,h,n,dx); row=np.empty(n+1); row[:n]=g; row[n]=-1; A_rows.append(row); b.append(-S[ix])
   A_ub=np.vstack(A_rows); b_ub=np.array(b)
   A_eq=np.ones((1,n))
   bounds=[]; for i in range(n): lo=-min(h[i], trust); hi=min(1-h[i], trust); bounds.append((lo,hi)); bounds.append((None, 2.0))
   c=np.zeros(n+1); c[n]=1
   res=linprog(c, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=np.zeros(1), bounds=bounds, method='HiGHS')
   if not res.success: return h,M,False
   p=res.x[:n]
   # If p all zero or target not lower, maybe still line search
   # Try alphas
   alphas=[1.0,0.5,0.25,0.125,0.0625,0.03125]
   best=None; bestC=M
   for a in alphas:
      ht = h + a*p
      ht=project_feasible(ht,n)
      Ct=compute_c5(ht,n)
      if Ct < bestC:
         best=ht; bestC=Ct
   if best is not None: return best,bestC,True
   return h,M,False

This tries α and chooses best. If α=1 p yields C lower but α=0.5 lower? choose best. If p is improved but no alpha improves due projection, fail.

Implementation of SLSQP call:
def slsqp_smooth(h,beta,maxiter,ftol=1e-10):
   n=h.size; dx=2.0/n
   bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
   cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':sum_eq_fun,'jac':sum_eq_jac}]
   try:
      res=minimize(fun=smooth_fun, x0=h, args=(beta,dx), method='SLSQP',
                   jac=smooth_jac, bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                   options={'maxiter':maxiter, 'ftol':ftol, 'disp':False})
      return project_feasible(res.x,n)
   except Exception:
      return project_feasible(h,n)
`smooth_jac` returns array; good.

Need define `polish_continuous`:
def polish_continuous(h,n,deadline,light=False):
  h=project_feasible(h,n)
  c=compute_c5(h,n)
  best_h=h.copy();best_c=c
  # LP loop
  step=0.25 if not light else 0.1
  min_improve=0
  for _ in range(12 if not light else 6):
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     hnew,cnew=lp_minmax_step(h,n,step, active_count=120 if n>350 else 180)
     if cnew < best_c - 1e-10:
        h=best_h=hnew; c=best_c=cnew; step=max(step*0.85,0.01)
     else:
        step*=0.5
        if step < 0.005: break
  # SLSQP alphas
  alphas = [50,150,500,2000,8000] if not light else [50,300,1500]
  maxiter = 100 if not light else 40
  for beta in alphas:
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     h_try=slsqp_smooth(h,beta,maxiter=maxiter,ftol=1e-11)
     c_try=compute_c5(h_try,n)
     if c_try < c - 1e-12:
        h=h_try; best_h=h.copy(); c=best_h? best_c=c_try
     # continue from improved h regardless? If not improve, maybe still h changed? slsqp returns maybe worse; don't use if worse to avoid deception. Use h if c_try < c else keep.
  # Optional second LP after SLSQP if time
  step=0.08
  for _ in range(6):
     if time.time()>deadline: break
     hnew,cnew=lp_minmax_step(h,n,step, active_count=150)
     if cnew < c - 1e-10:
        h=hnew; c=cnew; best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c; step*=0.8
     else:
        step*=0.5
        if step<0.003: break
  return best_h,best_c,n

def binary_ensemble(h_best,n,deadline,seed):
  if n%2: n=2*(n//2); h_best=resample...
  best_h=None; best_c=inf
  # start from best
  h0=to_binary(h_best,n)
  hb,cb=binary_greedy(h0,n, deadline*0.5, seed)
  update
  # start from perturbed best if time
  if time < deadline*0.75:
    hp = project_feasible(h_best + 0.1*rng.randn(n), n)
    h0=to_binary(hp,n)
    hb,cb=binary_greedy(h0,n, deadline*0.75, seed+1)
  # start from random if time
   if time < deadline*0.95:
    h0=random_binary(n, rng)
    hb,cb=binary_greedy(h0,n, deadline, seed+2)
  return best_h,best_c,n

def add_term_weighted(G, idx, x, n, weight):
   if weight ==0: return
   ell0=n-1
   if idx==ell0:
      G += weight*(1-2*x)
   elif idx>ell0:
      ell=idx-ell0; left=n-ell
      G[:left] += weight*(1.0 - x[ell:])
      G[ell:] -= weight*x[:left]
   else:
      ell=ell0-idx; left=n-ell
      G[ell:] += weight*(1.0 - x[:left])
      G[:left] -= weight*x[ell:]
This loops active terms. For active count small, good.

Now, potential issue with `project_feasible`: The algorithm `h[mask] *= (h.sum()+diff)/h.sum()` for diff negative can over/under due denominator. Let's write robust:
def project_feasible(h,n):
  h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  target=n/2.0
  for _ in range(200):
    s=h.sum(); diff=target-s
    if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
    if diff>0:
      room=1-h
      mask=room>1e-15
      if not mask.any(): break
      total_room=room[mask].sum()
      if total_room<=0: break
      # scale room to add exactly diff, but cap by room
      h[mask] += (diff/total_room)*room[mask]
    else:
      mask=h>1e-15
      if not mask.any(): break
      total=h[mask].sum()
      h[mask] += (diff/total)*h[mask]
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  # final greedy correction
  diff=target-h.sum()
  if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    if diff>0:
      order=np.argsort(1-h)
    else:
      order=np.argsort(h)
    for idx in order:
      if diff>0:
        add=min(diff,1-h[idx])
        h[idx]+=add; diff-=add
        if diff<=1e-15: break
      else:
        sub=min(-diff,h[idx])
        h[idx]-=sub; diff+=sub
        if diff>=-1e-15: break
  h=np.clip(h,0,1)
  return h
This should work. If target infeasible due all 0/1 sum not target? But if all 0/1 and sum target not possible? n integer target? If h binary with wrong count, room total maybe n-sum, can add. OK.

Now, `compute_c5`:
def compute_c5(h,n):
  if n <=0: return 1.0
  dx=2.0/n
  c=np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')*dx
  return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
If h contains nan, return inf? Could sanitize: if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)): return inf. In run, if C inf, revert. We'll add in `safe_update`:

    return h,best? We'll just inline.

Candidate selection:
```
base = D.max(axis=1)
cand = np.nonzero(base < M - 1e-9)[0]
if cand.size:
  if cand.size > 120:
    order_c = cand[np.argsort(base[cand])[:120]]
  else: order_c = cand
  best_cand=None; best_val=M
  for ci in order_c:
    row = D[ci]
    j = ones[int(ci)]
    d = abs(i-j)
    idxP=n-1+d; idxN=n-1-d
    # row is view into D, D = B - Vones; base=row.max()
    # correction could make row idx values +1
    val = row.max()
    vp = row[idxP] + 1.0
    if vp > val: val=vp
    vn = row[idxN] + 1.0
    if vn > val: val=vn
    if val < best_val:
       best_val=val; best_cand=ci
  if best_cand is not None:
    ci=int(best_cand); j=ones[ci]
    row=D[ci].copy()
    d=abs(i-j); idxP...; row[idxP]+=1; row[idxN]+=1
    C = np.rint(row)
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
  t0=time.time()
  total=max(1.0,budget_s-15)
  hard=t0+budget_s-5
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  # init
  try: h0=...
  except: ...

  n0=h0.size
  h0=project_feasible(h0,n0)
  best_h,best_c=refresh_solution(h0,n0)
  best_n=n0
  if not np.isfinite(best_c): ...
  # Phase A
  time_A_end=t0 + min(total*0.33, 280.0)
  if time_A_end>t0:
    hA,cA,nA=polish_continuous(best_h,best_n,time_A_end, light=False)
    HA,CA=refresh_solution(hA,nA); update
  # Phase B binary
  time_B_end=time_A_end + min(total*0.27, 240.0)
  if best_n%2==0 and time.time()<time_B_end and np.isfinite(best_c):
    hB,cB,nB=binary_ensemble(best_h,best_n,time_B_end,seed)

  # Phase C other res

  if time.time()<time_C_end:
     resolutions=[200,260,320,420,520]

        if nh==best_n: continue

        hh=resample_h(best_h,best_n,nh)

           if nh%2==0:

  # Phase D final

  # final safe

  best_h=project_feasible(best_h,best_n)
  best_c=compute_c5(best_h,best_n)
  return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize, linprog


def project_feasible(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
        h = np.resize(h, n)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.5)
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    target = 0.5 * n

    for _ in range(100):
        s = float(h.sum())
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            mask = room > 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            tot = float(room[mask].sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / tot) * room[mask]
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-15
            if not np.any(mask):
                break
            tot = float(h[mask].sum())
            if tot <= 0.0:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / tot) * h[mask]

    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    diff = target - float(h.sum())
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            order = np.argsort(1.0 - h)
        else:
            order = np.argsort(h)
        for idx in order:
            idx = int(idx)
            if diff > 0.0:
                add = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
                h[idx] += add
                diff -= add
                if diff <= 1e-15:
                    break
            else:
                sub = min(-diff, h[idx])
                h[idx] -= sub
                diff += sub
                if diff >= -1e-15:
                    break
    h = np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0)
    return h


def compute_c5(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n <= 0 or h.size < n:
        return float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
        return float("inf")
    dx = 2.0 / n
    c = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
    return float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0


def refresh_solution(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    return h, c


def resample_h(h, n, nn):
    if nn == n:
        return project_feasible(h, n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).ravel()
    if n <= 0:
        out = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, nn))
        return project_feasible(out, nn)
    xo = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n, endpoint=True)
    xn = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, nn, endpoint=True)
    hh = np.interp(xn, xo, h)
    return project_feasible(hh, nn)


def sum_eq_fun(x):
    return float(x.sum() - 0.5 * x.size)


def sum_eq_jac(x):
    return np.ones_like(x)


def add_term_weighted(G, idx, x, n, weight):
    if weight == 0.0:
        return
    ell0 = n - 1
    if idx == ell0:
        G += weight * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
    elif idx > ell0:
        ell = idx - ell0
        left = n - ell
        G[:left] += weight * (1.0 - x[ell:])
        G[ell:] -= weight * x[:left]
    else:
        ell = ell0 - idx
        left = n - ell
        G[ell:] += weight * (1.0 - x[:left])
        G[:left] -= weight * x[ell:]


def smooth_compute(x, beta, dx):
    n = int(x.size)
    S = np.correlate(x, 1.0 - x, mode="full") * dx
    C = float(np.max(S))
    shift = beta * (S - C)
    shift = np.where(shift < -745.0, -745.0, shift)
    e = np.exp(shift)
    sume = float(e.sum())
    if sume <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(sume):
        e = np.zeros_like(S)
        int(np.argmax(S))
        e[int(np.argmax(S))] = 1.0
        sume = 1.0
    w = e / sume
    val = C + np.log(sume) / beta

    G = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    active = np.nonzero(w > 1e-10)[0]
    ell0 = n - 1

    if active.size < 80:
        for idx in active:
            add_term_weighted(G, int(idx), x, n, float(w[int(idx)]))
    else:
        G += w[ell0] * (1.0 - 2.0 * x)
        for ell in range(1, n):
            wp = float(w[ell0 + ell])
            wn = float(w[ell0 - ell])
            c = wp + wn
            if c == 0.0:
                continue
            left = n - ell
            G[:left] += wp - c * x[ell:]
            G[ell:] += wn - c * x[:left]

    G *= dx
    return float(val), G


def smooth_fun(x, beta, dx):
    val, _ = smooth_compute(x, beta, dx)
    return val


def smooth_jac(x, beta, dx):
    _, g = smooth_compute(x, beta, dx)
    return g


def slsqp_smooth(h, beta, maxiter, ftol=1e-11):
    h = project_feasible(h, h.size)
    n = int(h.size)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = ({"type": "eq", "fun": sum_eq_fun, "jac": sum_eq_jac},)
    try:
        res = minimize(
            fun=smooth_fun,
            x0=h,
            args=(float(beta), float(dx)),
            method="SLSQP",
            jac=smooth_jac,
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": float(ftol), "disp": False},
        )
        if res is not None and hasattr(res, "x") and res.x is not None:
            return project_feasible(res.x, n)
    except Exception:
        pass
    return h


def term_grad(idx, h, n, dx):
    g = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    add_term_weighted(g, int(idx), h, n, 1.0)
    g *= dx
    return g


def lp_minmax_step(h, n, trust, active_count=120):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    n = int(n)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    S = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx
    M = float(np.max(S))

    band = max(3.0 * dx + 1e-6, 0.004)
    near = np.nonzero(S >= M - band)[0]
    ac = int(min(max(active_count, 16), 240))
    if near.size > ac:
        idx = np.argsort(S)[-ac:]
    elif near.size > 0:
        idx = near
    else:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(S))])

    imax = int(np.argmax(S))
    if imax not in idx:
        idx = np.append(idx, imax)
    idx = np.unique(idx)
    if idx.size > 2 * ac:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(S[idx])[-ac:]]

    A_ub = np.empty((idx.size, n + 1), dtype=float)
    b_ub = np.empty(idx.size, dtype=float)
    for k, ix in enumerate(idx):
        g = term_grad(ix, h, n, dx)
        A_ub[k, :n] = g
        A_ub[k, n] = -1.0
        b_ub[k] = -float(S[ix])

    A_eq = np.ones((1, n), dtype=float)
    b_eq = np.array([0.0])
    bounds = []
    for i in range(n):
        lo = -min(float(h[i]), float(trust))
        hi = min(1.0 - float(h[i]), float(trust))
        bounds.append((lo, hi))
    bounds.append((None, 2.0))
    c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=float)
    c_obj[n] = 1.0

    try:
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_ub,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
            )
        except Exception:
            res = linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A_ub, b_ub=b_ub, A_eq=A_eq, b_eq=b_eq, bounds=bounds)
    except Exception:
        return h, M, False

    if res is None or not getattr(res, "success", False) or res.x is None:
        return h, M, False

    p = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=float)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
        return h, M, False

    base = h.copy()
    best = None
    best_c = M
    for a in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125, 0.0625, 0.03125, 0.015625):
        ht = base + a * p
        ht = project_feasible(ht, n)
        ct = compute_c5(ht, n)
        if np.isfinite(ct) and ct < best_c:
            best = ht
            best_c = ct
    if best is None:
        return h, M, False
    return best, float(best_c), True


def polish_continuous(h, n, deadline, light=False):
    if time.time() >= float(deadline):
        h = project_feasible(h, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n), n

    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    n = int(n)
    c = compute_c5(h, n)
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = float(c)

    act = 80 if n > 380 else 120
    step = 0.12 if light else 0.20
    lp_rounds = 5 if light else 12
    for _ in range(lp_rounds):
        if time.time() >= float(deadline) - 2.0:
            break
        h2, c2, ok = lp_minmax_step(h, n, step, active_count=act)
        if ok and np.isfinite(c2) and c2 < best_c - 1e-11:
            h = h2
            c = float(c2)
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c
            step = max(step * 0.85, 0.006)
        else:
            step *= 0.55
            if step < 0.004:
                break

    alphas = (60, 200, 800, 3000) if light else (30, 100, 300, 1000, 3000)
    maxiter = 35 if light else (70 if n <= 320 else 55)
    for beta in alphas:
        if time.time() >= float(deadline) - 8.0:
            break
        h3 = slsqp_smooth(h, float(beta), maxiter, 1e-11)
        c3 = compute_c5(h3, n)
        if np.isfinite(c3) and c3 < c - 1e-12:
            h = h3
            c = float(c3)
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c

    step = 0.05 if light else 0.09
    post_rounds = 4 if light else 8
    for _ in range(post_rounds):
        if time.time() >= float(deadline) - 2.0:
            break
        h4, c4, ok = lp_minmax_step(h, n, step, active_count=act)
        if ok and np.isfinite(c4) and c4 < c - 1e-11:
            h = h4
            c = float(c4)
            best_h = h.copy()
            best_c = c
            step = max(step * 0.8, 0.004)
        else:
            step *= 0.5
            if step < 0.0025:
                break

    return best_h, float(best_c), n


def to_binary(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n -= 1
        h = project_feasible(h[:n], n)
    else:
        n = int(n)
    hb = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    hb[np.argsort(h)[::-1][: n // 2]] = 1.0
    return hb


def random_binary(n, rng):
    n = int(n)
    m = n // 2
    h = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if m > 0:
        h[rng.choice(n, size=m, replace=False)] = 1.0
    return h


def compute_flip_vectors(h, n):
    n = int(n)
    m = 2 * n - 1
    V = np.zeros((n, m), dtype=float)
    ell0 = n - 1
    for ell in range(1, n):
        left = n - ell
        posP = ell0 + ell
        posN = ell0 - ell
        hr = h[ell:]
        hl = h[:left]
        V[:left, posP] += 1.0 - hr
        V[:left, posN] -= hr
        V[ell:, posP] -= hl
        V[ell:, posN] += 1.0 - hl
    return V


def binary_greedy(h_init, n, deadline, seed=42):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n -= 1
        h_init = project_feasible(h_init, n)
    if n < 6:
        h = to_binary(h_init, n)
        return h, compute_c5(h, n), n

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    h = to_binary(h_init, n)
    C = np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full").astype(float)
    C = np.rint(C)
    M = float(C.max())
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_h = h.copy()
    best_c = M * dx
    ones = [int(x) for x in np.where(h == 1.0)[0]]
    zeros = [int(x) for x in np.where(h == 0.0)[0]]

    while time.time() < float(deadline):
        if not ones or not zeros:
            break
        V = compute_flip_vectors(h, n)
        Vones = V[ones]
        order = zeros[:]
        rng.shuffle(order)

        improved = False
        for i in order:
            if time.time() >= float(deadline) - 1.0:
                break
            if i not in set(zeros) or not ones:
                continue
            B = C + V[i]
            if Vones.shape[0] == 0:
                break
            D = B[None, :] - Vones
            base = D.max(axis=1)
            cand = np.nonzero(base < M - 1e-9)[0]
            if cand.size == 0:
                continue
            if cand.size > 140:
                cand = cand[np.argsort(base[cand])[:140]]

            best_val = M
            best_ci = None
            for ci in cand:
                ci = int(ci)
                row = D[ci]
                j = ones[ci]
                d = abs(i - j)
                idxP = n - 1 + d
                idxN = n - 1 - d
                val = float(row.max())
                v1 = float(row[idxP]) + 1.0
                v2 = float(row[idxN]) + 1.0
                if v1 > val:
                    val = v1
                if v2 > val:
                    val = v2
                if val < best_val:
                    best_val = val
                    best_ci = ci

            if best_ci is None:
                continue
            j = ones[best_ci]
            row = D[best_ci].copy()
            d = abs(i - j)
            idxP = n - 1 + d
            idxN = n - 1 - d
            row[idxP] += 1.0
            row[idxN] += 1.0
            rowc = np.rint(row)
            M2 = float(rowc.max())
            if M2 < M - 1e-12:
                C = rowc
                M = M2
                h[i] = 1.0
                h[j] = 0.0
                try:
                    ones.remove(j)
                except Exception:
                    ones = [int(x) for x in np.where(h == 1.0)[0]]
                    zeros = [int(x) for x in np.where(h == 0.0)[0]]
                    ones.append(i)
                    zeros.append(j)
                else:
                    try:
                        zeros.remove(i)
                    except Exception:
                        zeros = [int(x) for x in np.where(h == 0.0)[0]]
                    ones.append(i)
                    zeros.append(j)

                if M * dx < best_c:
                    best_c = M * dx
                    best_h = h.copy()
                improved = True
                break

        if not improved:
            break

    h = project_feasible(best_h, n)
    return h, compute_c5(h, n), n


def binary_ensemble(h_best, n, deadline, seed=42):
    n = int(n)
    if n % 2 == 1:
        n -= 1
        h_best = project_feasible(h_best, n)
    if n < 6 or time.time() >= float(deadline):
        return h_best, compute_c5(h_best, n), int(n)

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    best_h = None
    best_c = float("inf")

    def better(h, c, nn):
        nonlocal best_h, best_c
        if c < best_c:
            best_h = h
            best_c = c

    h1, c1, _ = binary_greedy(h_best, n, min(float(deadline), time.time() + 0.55 * (float(deadline) - time.time())), seed)
    better(h1, c1, n)

    if time.time() < float(deadline) - 20.0:
        hp = project_feasible(h_best + 0.08 * rng.standard_normal(n), n)
        h2, c2, _ = binary_greedy(
            hp,
            n,
            min(float(deadline), time.time() + 0.25 * (float(deadline) - time.time())),
            seed + 1,
        )
        better(h2, c2, n)

    if time.time() < float(deadline) - 15.0:
        hr = random_binary(n, rng)
        h3, c3, _ = binary_greedy(
            hr,
            n,
            min(float(deadline), time.time() + 0.20 * (float(deadline) - time.time())),
            seed + 7,
        )
        better(h3, c3, n)

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = h_best
        best_c = compute_c5(h_best, n)
    return best_h, float(best_c), n


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    hard_deadline = t0 + max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 5.0)
    total = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 15.0)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    try:
        h0 = np.array(initial_h_values, dtype=float).ravel().copy()
    except Exception:
        n0 = 400
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n0, endpoint=True)
        h0 = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)

    if h0.size == 0:
        n0 = 400
        h0 = np.full(n0, 0.5)
    else:
        n0 = int(h0.size)

    if n0 % 2 == 1:
        n0 += 1
        h0 = resample_h(h0, n0 - 1, n0)

    best_h, best_c = refresh_solution(h0, n0)
    best_n = n0
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = np.full(n0, 0.5)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, n0)

    # Main continuous polish of the supplied parent.
    time_A_end = t0 + min(0.33 * total, 300.0)
    time_A_end = min(time_A_end, hard_deadline - 60.0)
    if time.time() < time_A_end:
        hA, cA, nA = polish_continuous(best_h, best_n, time_A_end, light=False)
        hA, cA = refresh_solution(hA, nA)
        if np.isfinite(cA) and cA < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hA, cA, nA

    # Binary local search at the current resolution.
    time_B_end = min(time_A_end + min(0.28 * total, 250.0), hard_deadline - 80.0)
    if best_n % 2 == 0 and time.time() < time_B_end:
        hB, cB, nB = binary_ensemble(best_h, best_n, time_B_end, seed + 101)
        hB, cB = refresh_solution(hB, nB)
        if np.isfinite(cB) and cB < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hB, cB, nB

    # Explore other sample counts.
    time_C_end = min(time_B_end + min(0.32 * total, 270.0), hard_deadline - 70.0)
    resolutions = [200, 260, 320, 420, 520]
    if time.time() < time_C_end:
        for nh in resolutions:
            if nh == best_n:
                continue
            if time.time() > time_C_end - 35.0:
                break
            hh = resample_h(best_h, best_n, nh)
            dur = max(25.0, min(90.0, (time_C_end - time.time()) * 0.55))
            end_c = min(time_C_end, time.time() + dur)
            hq, cq, nq = polish_continuous(hh, nh, end_c, light=True)
            hq, cq = refresh_solution(hq, nq)
            if np.isfinite(cq) and cq < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hq, cq, nq
            if nq % 2 == 0 and time.time() < time_C_end - 15.0:
                end_b = min(time_C_end, time.time() + min(30.0, dur * 0.45))
                hb, cb, nb = binary_ensemble(hq, nq, end_b, seed + 500 + nh)
                hb, cb = refresh_solution(hb, nb)
                if np.isfinite(cb) and cb < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, cb, nb

    # Final polish.
    if time.time() < hard_deadline - 20.0:
        light_final = best_n > 420 or (hard_deadline - time.time()) < 160.0
        hD, cD, nD = polish_continuous(best_h, best_n, hard_deadline - 3.0, light=light_final)
        hD, cD = refresh_solution(hD, nD)
        if np.isfinite(cD) and cD < best_c:
            best_h, best_c, best_n = hD, cD, nD

    # If still above target and time remains, try a few direct binary restarts.
    if np.isfinite(best_c) and best_c > 0.38085 and time.time() < hard_deadline - 45.0:
        for nh in (200, 256, 320):
            if nh == best_n or time.time() > hard_deadline - 25.0:
                continue
            hr = random_binary(nh, rng)
            endb = min(hard_deadline - 5.0, time.time() + 0.4 * (hard_deadline - time.time()))
            hb, cb, nb = binary_greedy(hr, nh, endb, seed + 900 + nh)
            hb, cb = refresh_solution(hb, nb)
            if np.isfinite(cb) and cb < best_c:
                best_h, best_c, best_n = hb, cb, nb

    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = np.full(best_n if best_n > 0 else 400, 0.5)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)

    best_h = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n)
    if not np.isfinite(best_c):
        best_h = np.full(max(4, n0), 0.5)
        best_c = compute_c5(best_h, best_n if best_n > 0 else int(best_h.size))
        best_n = int(best_h.size)

    return best_h, float(best_c), int(best_n)
```
